## 指定欄位的屬性域 (資料管理工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 屬性域工具集

**english**　Assign Domain To Field

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/assign-domain-to-field.html

**id**　15bdfdb3fd92ea9293-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/assign-domain-to-field.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Assign Domain To Field，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入表 [in_table]、欄位名 [field_name]、屬性域名稱 [domain_name]、子類型 （可選） [subtype_code]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/assign-domain-to-field.html#parameters

可以使用此工具來修改電信域網絡的電路屬性。 配置參數用于定義相應電路屬性，當創建或修改電路，以及在驗證、導出和追蹤過程中運行追蹤時需要支持這些電路屬性。

## 設置電路屬性 (公共設施網絡工具) · 用法

**id**　15c348673d4fc3a4a6-2

**english**　Set Circuit Properties

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱
- 管理工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-circuit-properties.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-circuit-properties.html

**text**　此工具用于設置和更改電路屬性。 要改變電信域網絡的電路屬性的值，請修改參數值，然后再次運行該工具。
公共設施網絡資料集必須為 公共設施網絡 8 或更高版本，并且包含電信域網絡。
必須 禁用 網絡拓撲。
使用企業級地理資料庫時，必須滿足以下要求： 活動 ArcGIS Enterprise 門戶賬戶必須為 門戶公共設施網絡所有者。 輸入公共設施網絡參數值必須來自作為 資料庫公共設施網絡所有者 建立的資料庫連接。 此工具必須連接到默認版本才能運行。 在啟用網絡拓撲操作期間，將阻止連接到默認版本的所有其他會話運行驗證或啟用操作。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-circuit-properties.html#usage

- 標注
- 說明

- 指定如果遇到非空間交匯點或邊對象并且遍歷對象的關聯層次結構中沒有必要的包含、附件或連通性關聯，是否將追蹤操作期間返回錯誤。 此參數確保可以通過與圖徵或其他可定位對象的關聯來定位追蹤操作返回的非空間對象。 選中—如果遇到非空間交匯點或邊對象并且遍歷對象的關聯層次結構中沒有必要的包含、附件或連通性關聯，將返回一條錯誤。 未選中—追蹤不會檢查是否存在無法定位的對象并返回結果，無論遍歷對象的關聯層次結構中是否存在無法定位的對象。 這是默認設置。

## 設置電路屬性 (公共設施網絡工具) · 參數

**id**　15c348673d4fc3a4a6-14

**english**　Set Circuit Properties

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱
- 管理工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-circuit-properties.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-circuit-properties.html

**parameter**　summaries

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 匯總 （可選）
- 驗證電路時，將存儲函數結果的 Summary Attribute 欄位。 可以根據組成電路的所有圖徵來計算匯總資料。 可以過濾計算以僅應用于具有特定屬性值或類別的網絡圖徵。 注: 此參數目前尚未實現，保留供后續使用。 值表列： Function—Choose from a number of calculation functions. Minimum—The minimum of the input values. Maximum—The maximum of the input values. Average—The sum of the input values. Count—The average of the input values. Add—The number of features. Subtract—The difference between the input values. Attribute—Filter by any network attribute defined in the system. Filter Name—Filter the function results by attribute name. Filter Operator—Choose from a number of operators. Is equal to—The attribute is equal to the value. Does not equal—The attribute is not equal to the value. Is greater than—The attribute is greater than the value. Is greater than or equal to—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. Is less than—The attribute is less than the value. Is less than or equal to—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. Includes the values (bitwise AND equals the value)—bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). Does not include the values (bitwise AND does not equal the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). Includes any (bitwise AND equals True)—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). Does not include any (bitwise AND equals False)—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). Filter Type—Choose from a number of filter types. Specific value—Filter by a specific value. Network attribute—Filter by a network attribute. Filter Value—Provide a specific value for the input filter attribute. Summary Attribute—The field in the Circuit table that will persist the function result. Depending on the specified function and network attribute type, only the applicable type of user-added attributes will be valid for this parameter. If a field to store the summary result does not exist in the Circuit table, the Add Field tool can be used to create one. A field can only support the result of one summary; each summary requires its own field in the Circuit table. See the Usages section for a matrix of valid field types for the summary attribute field based on the specified function. Learn more about bitwise operators
- Value Table

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-circuit-properties.html#parameters

## 設置電路屬性 (公共設施網絡工具) · 參數

**id**　15c348673d4fc3a4a6-15

**english**　Set Circuit Properties

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱
- 管理工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-circuit-properties.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-circuit-properties.html

**parameter**　condition_barriers

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 條件障礙 （可選）
- 基于與網絡屬性的比較或對類別字符串的檢查，將對圖徵設置的可遍歷性障礙條件。 條件障礙使用網絡屬性、運算符和類型以及屬性值。 例如，當圖徵的 Device Status 屬性等于特定值 Open 時，將停止追蹤。 當圖徵滿足此條件時，追蹤將停止。 如果您要使用多個屬性，可使用“組合方法”列來定義 And 或 Or 條件。 值表列： Name—Filter by any network attribute or network category defined in the system. Operator—Choose from a number of operators. Is equal to—The attribute is equal to the value. Does not equal—The attribute is not equal to the value. Is greater than—The attribute is greater than the value. Is greater than or equal to—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. Is less than—The attribute is less than the value. Is less than or equal to—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. Includes the values (bitwise AND equals the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). Does not include the values (bitwise AND does not equal the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). Includes any (bitwise AND equals True)—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). Does not include any (bitwise AND equals False)—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). Type—Choose a specific value or network attribute from the name value. Specific value—Filter by a specific value or network category. Network attribute—Filter by a network attribute. Value—Provide a specific value for the input attribute type that would cause termination based on the operator value. Combine Using—Set this value if you have multiple attributes to add. You can combine them using an And or an Or condition. And—Combine the condition barriers. Or—Filter by a network attribute.
- Value Table

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-circuit-properties.html#parameters

**values**　
- 函數障礙 （可選）
- 基于函數對圖徵設置可遍歷性障礙。 函數障礙可用于執行以下操作：限制追蹤距離起點的行程或設置停止追蹤的最大值。 例如，所經過的每條線的長度和為目前經過的總距離。 當經過的總長度達到指定值時，追蹤將停止。 值表列： Function—Choose from a number of calculation functions. Minimum—The minimum of the input values. Maximum—The maximum of the input values. Average—The average of the input values. Count—The number of features. Add—The sum of the input values Subtract—The difference between the input values. Attribute—Filter by any network attribute defined in the system. Operator—Choose from a number of operators. Is equal to—The attribute is equal to the value. Does not equal—The attribute is not equal to the value. Is greater than—The attribute is greater than the value. Is greater than or equal to—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. Is less than—The attribute is less than the value. Is less than or equal to—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. Includes the values (bitwise AND equals the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). Does not include the values (bitwise AND does not equal the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). Includes any (bitwise AND equals True)—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). Does not include any (bitwise AND equals False)—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). Value—Provide a specific value of the input attribute type that, if discovered, will cause the termination. Use Local Values—Calculate values in each direction as opposed to an overall global value. For example, use for a function barrier that is calculating the sum of a shape length in which the trace terminates if the value is greater than or equal to 4. In the global case, after you have traversed two edges with a value of 2, you have already reached a shape length sum of 4, so the trace stops. If local values are used, the local values along each path change, and the trace continues.
- Value Table

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-circuit-properties.html#parameters

## 設置電路屬性 (公共設施網絡工具) · 參數

**id**　15c348673d4fc3a4a6-17

**english**　Set Circuit Properties

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱
- 管理工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-circuit-properties.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-circuit-properties.html

**parameter**　traversability_scope

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 應用可遍歷性至 （可選）
- 指定將應用的可遍歷性類型。 可遍歷性范圍用于確定是否在交匯點和/或邊處應用可遍歷性。 例如，如果定義了用于停止追蹤的條件障礙，其中 Device Status 等于 Open 并將遍歷范圍僅設置為邊，則即使追蹤遇到開路設備，追蹤也不會停止，因為 Device Status 僅適用于交匯點。 換言之，此參數會為追蹤指明是否要忽略交匯點和/或邊。 交匯點和邊—可遍歷性將同時應用于交匯點和邊。 這是默認設置。 僅交匯點—可遍歷性將僅應用于交匯點。 僅邊—可遍歷性將僅應用于邊。
- String

**english**　Set Circuit Properties

**parameter**　share_circuit_locations

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- share_circuit_locations （可選）
- 指定是否可在電路之間共享用于定義電路起點和終點位置的圖徵。 這適用于定義電路的所有起始和停止位置。 必須將交匯點或交匯點對象分配為電路位置 網絡類別，作為電路或電路段的起始或終止位置；但是，線或邊對象不需要電路位置網絡類別，當其起點和終點端并非兩側均有連接時，可以作為起始或終止位置。 SHARE_CIRCUIT_LOCATIONS—圖徵或對象可用于定義多個電路 DO_NOT_SHARE_CIRCUIT_LOCATIONS—圖徵或對象只能用于定義單個電路。 這是默認設置。
- Boolean

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-circuit-properties.html#derived-output

**interface**　python

- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- import_circuits_as_clean （可選）
- 指定回路定義將以“干凈”還是“臟”狀態導入到電路表中。 IMPORT_CIRCUITS_AS_CLEAN—創建已導入電路，其中 STATUS = Clean 并且 LASTVERIFIED = null。 當禁用網絡拓撲時，將不會更新 STATUS 欄位，并且電路保持干凈狀態。 IMPORT_CIRCUITS_AS_DIRTY—創建已導入電路，其中 STATUS = Dirty。 如果禁用網絡拓撲，則會將所有電路的 STATUS 欄位更新為 Dirty。 這是默認設置。
- Boolean

**source_language**　zh-cn

**values**　
- validate_locatability （可選）
- 指定如果遇到非空間交匯點或邊對象并且遍歷對象的關聯層次結構中沒有必要的包含、附件或連通性關聯，是否將追蹤操作期間返回錯誤。 此參數確保可以通過與圖徵或其他可定位對象的關聯來定位追蹤操作返回的非空間對象。 VALIDATE_LOCATABILITY—如果遇到非空間交匯點或邊對象并且遍歷對象的關聯層次結構中沒有必要的包含、附件或連通性關聯，將返回一條錯誤。 DO_NOT_VALIDATE_LOCATABILITY—追蹤不會檢查是否存在無法定位的對象并返回結果，無論遍歷對象的關聯層次結構中是否存在無法定位的對象。 這是默認設置。
- Boolean

## 設置電路屬性 (公共設施網絡工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　15c348673d4fc3a4a6-32

**english**　Set Circuit Properties

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱
- 管理工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-circuit-properties.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-circuit-properties.html

**parameter**　summaries

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- summaries [summaries,...] （可選）
- 驗證電路時，將存儲函數結果的 Summary Attribute 欄位。 可以根據組成電路的所有圖徵來計算匯總資料。 可以過濾計算以僅應用于具有特定屬性值或類別的網絡圖徵。 注: 此參數目前尚未實現，保留供后續使用。 值表列： Function—Choose from a number of calculation functions. MIN—The minimum of the input values. MAX—The maximum of the input values. AVERAGE—The sum of the input values. COUNT—The average of the input values. ADD—The number of features. SUBTRACT—The difference between the input values. Attribute—Filter by any network attribute defined in the system. name—Filter the function results by attribute name. operator—Choose from a number of operators. IS_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is equal to the value. DOES_NOT_EQUAL—The attribute is not equal to the value. IS_GREATER_THAN—The attribute is greater than the value. IS_GREATER_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. IS_LESS_THAN—The attribute is less than the value. IS_LESS_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. INCLUDES_THE_VALUES—bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_THE_VALUES—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). INCLUDES_ANY—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_ANY—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). type—Choose from a number of filter types. SPECIFIC_VALUE—Filter by a specific value. NETWORK_ATTRIBUTE—Filter by a network attribute. Filter Value—Provide a specific value for the input filter attribute. Summary Attribute—The field in the Circuit table that will persist the function result. Depending on the specified function and network attribute type, only the applicable type of user-added attributes will be valid for this parameter. If a field to store the summary result does not exist in the Circuit table, the Add Field tool can be used to create one. A field can only support the result of one summary; each summary requires its own field in the Circuit table. See the Usages section for a matrix of valid field types for the summary attribute field based on the specified function. Learn more about bitwise operators
- Value Table

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-circuit-properties.html#derived-output

## 設置電路屬性 (公共設施網絡工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　15c348673d4fc3a4a6-33

**english**　Set Circuit Properties

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱
- 管理工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-circuit-properties.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-circuit-properties.html

**parameter**　condition_barriers

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- condition_barriers [[[Name, Operator, Type, Value, Combine Using],...] （可選）
- 基于與網絡屬性的比較或對類別字符串的檢查，將對圖徵設置的可遍歷性障礙條件。 條件障礙使用網絡屬性、運算符和類型以及屬性值。 例如，當圖徵的 Device Status 屬性等于特定值 Open 時，將停止追蹤。 當圖徵滿足此條件時，追蹤將停止。 如果您要使用多個屬性，可使用“組合方法”列來定義 And 或 Or 條件。 值表列： Name—Filter by any network attribute or network category defined in the system. Operator—Choose from a number of operators. IS_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is equal to the value. DOES_NOT_EQUAL—The attribute is not equal to the value. IS_GREATER_THAN—The attribute is greater than the value. IS_GREATER_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. IS_LESS_THAN—The attribute is less than the value. IS_LESS_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. INCLUDES_THE_VALUES—A bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_THE_VALUES—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). INCLUDES_ANY—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_ANY—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). Type—Choose a specific value or network attribute from the name value. SPECIFIC_VALUE—Filter by a specific value or network category. NETWORK_ATTRIBUTE—Filter by a network attribute. Value—Provide a specific value for the input attribute type that would cause termination based on the operator value. Combine Using—Set this value if you have multiple attributes to add. You can combine them using an And or an Or condition. AND—Combine the condition barriers. OR—Filter by a network attribute.
- Value Table

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-circuit-properties.html#derived-output

## 設置電路屬性 (公共設施網絡工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　15c348673d4fc3a4a6-34

**english**　Set Circuit Properties

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱
- 管理工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-circuit-properties.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-circuit-properties.html

**parameter**　function_barriers

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- function_barriers [function_barriers,...] （可選）
- 基于函數對圖徵設置可遍歷性障礙。 函數障礙可用于執行以下操作：限制追蹤距離起點的行程或設置停止追蹤的最大值。 例如，所經過的每條線的長度和為目前經過的總距離。 當經過的總長度達到指定值時，追蹤將停止。 值表列： Function—Choose from a number of calculation functions. MIN—The minimum of the input values. MAX—The maximum of the input values. AVERAGE—The average of the input values. COUNT—The number of features. ADD—The sum of the input values SUBTRACT—The difference between the input values. Attribute—Filter by any network attribute defined in the system. Operator—Choose from a number of operators. IS_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is equal to the value. DOES_NOT_EQUAL—The attribute is not equal to the value. IS_GREATER_THAN—The attribute is greater than the value. IS_GREATER_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. IS_LESS_THAN—The attribute is less than the value. IS_LESS_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. INCLUDES_THE_VALUES—A bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_THE_VALUES—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). INCLUDES_ANY—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_ANY—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). Value—Provide a specific value of the input attribute type that, if discovered, will cause the termination. Use Local Values—Calculate values in each direction as opposed to an overall global value. For example, use for a function barrier that is calculating the sum of a shape length in which the trace terminates if the value is greater than or equal to 4. In the global case, after you have traversed two edges with a value of 2, you have already reached a shape length sum of 4, so the trace stops. If local values are used, the local values along each path change, and the trace continues.
- Value Table

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-circuit-properties.html#derived-output

**parameter**　traversability_scope

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- traversability_scope （可選）
- 指定將應用的可遍歷性類型。 可遍歷性范圍用于確定是否在交匯點和/或邊處應用可遍歷性。 例如，如果定義了用于停止追蹤的條件障礙，其中 Device Status 等于 Open 并將遍歷范圍僅設置為邊，則即使追蹤遇到開路設備，追蹤也不會停止，因為 Device Status 僅適用于交匯點。 換言之，此參數會為追蹤指明是否要忽略交匯點和/或邊。 BOTH_JUNCTIONS_AND_EDGES—可遍歷性將同時應用于交匯點和邊。 這是默認設置。 JUNCTIONS_ONLY—可遍歷性將僅應用于交匯點。 EDGES_ONLY—可遍歷性將僅應用于邊。
- String

**source_language**　zh-cn

本示例將為電信域網絡 Telco 設置電路屬性。

## 設置電路屬性 (公共設施網絡工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　15c348673d4fc3a4a6-40

**english**　Set Circuit Properties

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱
- 管理工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-circuit-properties.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-circuit-properties.html

**text**　# Import required modules
import arcpy

# Set local variables
in_utility_network = "Telecom_Network"
domain_network = "Telco"
share_circuit_points = "SHARE_CIRCUIT_POINTS"
import_circuits_as_clean = "IMPORT_CIRCUITS_AS_DIRTY"
max_hops = 50
include_barriers = "INCLUDE_BARRIERS"
summaries = ""
condition_barriers = "'Status' IS_EQUAL_TO SPECIFIC_VALUE 'Proposed' #"
function_barriers = ""
traversability_scope = "BOTH_JUNCTIONS_AND_EDGES"
propagators="""Wavelengths # PROPAGATED_BITWISE_AND INCLUDES_THE_VALUES '"C27-C61"'""",
user_attributes="TelcoCircuit Owner;TelcoCircuitSection LesseeID;TelcoSubcircuit Lessee;TelcoSubcircuit LesseeID"

# Run the SetCircuitProperties tool
arcpy.un.SetCircuitProperties(in_utility_network,
                              domain_network,
                              share_circuit_points,
                              import_circuits_as_clean,
                              max_hops,
                              summaries,
                              condition_barriers,
                              function_barriers,
                              include_barriers,
                              traversability_scope,
                              propagators,
                              user_attributes)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-circuit-properties.html#derived-output

## 設置電路屬性 (公共設施網絡工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱
- 管理工具集

**english**　Set Circuit Properties

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-circuit-properties.html

**id**　15c348673d4fc3a4a6-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-circuit-properties.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Set Circuit Properties，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入公共設施網絡 [in_utility_network]、域網絡 [domain_network]、共享電路位置 （可選） [share_circuit_locations]、導入整潔電路資料 （可選） [import_circuits_as_clean]、最大跳數 （可選） [max_hops]、包括容器 （可選） [include_containers]、包括內容 （可選） [include_content]、包括結構 （可選） [include_structures]、包括障礙圖徵 （可選） [include_barriers]、驗證可定位性 （可選） [validate_locatability]、匯總 （可選） [summaries]、條件障礙 （可選） [condition_barriers]、函數障礙 （可選） [function_barriers]、應用可遍歷性至 （可選） [traversability_scope]、傳播程序 （可選） [propagators]、用戶屬性 （可選） [user_attributes]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-circuit-properties.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_utility_network", "label_zh": "輸入公共設施網絡", "label_en": "Input Utility Network", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Utility Network; Utility Network Layer", "description_en": "The input utility network that contains the circuit."}, {"parameter": "domain_network", "label_zh": "域網絡", "label_en": "Domain Network", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The telecom domain network that will be updated."}, {"parameter": "share_circuit_locations", "label_zh": "共享電路位置 （可選）", "label_en": "Share circuit locations (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the features that are used to define the start and stop locations for a circuit can be shared between circuits. This applies to all start and stop locations which define a circuit. A junction or junction object must be assigned the Circuit Location network category to serve as the start or stop location for a circuit or circuit section; however, a line or edge object does not require the Circuit Location network category and can serve as the start or stop location when it does not have connectivity on both the From and To sides. Checked—A feature or object can be used to define more than one circuit Unchecked—A feature or object can only be used to define a single circuit. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "import_circuits_as_clean", "label_zh": "導入整潔電路資料 （可選）", "label_en": "Import circuits as clean (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether circuit definitions will be imported to the Circuit table as Clean or Dirty. Checked—Imported circuits are created with STATUS = Clean and LASTVERIFIED = null. When the network topology is disabled, the STATUS field will not be updated, and circuits remain clean. Unchecked—Imported circuits are created with STATUS = Dirty. When the network topology is disabled, the STATUS field will be updated to Dirty for all circuits. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "max_hops", "label_zh": "最大跳數 （可選）", "label_en": "Max Hops (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "The maximum number of hops between edges that will be allowed in the path, excluding the start location. All edges and junction-junction connectivity associations are evaluated as having a fixed unit length of 1 on traversal. The default is 100."}, {"parameter": "include_containers", "label_zh": "包括容器 （可選）", "label_en": "Include containers (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether container features and objects traversed during the trace will be included in the trace results. Checked—Container features and objects will be included in the trace results. Unchecked—Container features and objects will not be included in the trace results. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "include_content", "label_zh": "包括內容 （可選）", "label_en": "Include content (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the trace will include content of containers traversed during the trace will be included in the trace results. Checked—Content of container features and objects will be included in the trace results. Unchecked—Content of container features and objects will not be included in the trace results. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "include_structures", "label_zh": "包括結構 （可選）", "label_en": "Include structures (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether structure features and objects with attachments to features traversed during the trace will be included in the trace results. Checked—Structure features and objects will be included in the trace results. Unchecked—Structure features and objects will not be included in the trace results. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "include_barriers", "label_zh": "包括障礙圖徵 （可選）", "label_en": "Include barrier features (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the traversability barrier features will be included in the trace results. Checked—Traversability barrier features will be included in the trace results. This is the default. Unchecked—Traversability barrier features will not be included in the trace results."}, {"parameter": "validate_locatability", "label_zh": "驗證可定位性 （可選）", "label_en": "Validate locatability (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether an error will be returned during a trace operation if nonspatial junction or edge objects are encountered without the necessary containment, attachment, or connectivity association in their association hierarchy of the traversed objects. This parameter ensures that nonspatial objects returned by a trace operation can be located through an association with features or other locatable objects. Checked—An error will be returned if nonspatial junction or edge objects are encountered without the necessary containment, attachment, or connectivity association in their association hierarchy of the traversed objects. Unchecked—The trace will not check for unlocatable objects and will return results regardless of whether unlocatable objects are present in the association hierarchy of the traversed objects. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "summaries", "label_zh": "匯總 （可選）", "label_en": "Summaries (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "The Summary Attribute field that will store function results when verifying a circuit. The summaries can be calculated on all the features that compose the circuit. Calculations can be filtered to apply only to network features with a particular attribute value or category. Note: This parameter is not currently implemented and is reserved for future work. Value table columns: Function—Choose from a number of calculation functions. Minimum—The minimum of the input values. Maximum—The maximum of the input values. Average—The sum of the input values. Count—The average of the input values. Add—The number of features. Subtract—The difference between the input values. Attribute—Filter by any network attribute defined in the system. Filter Name—Filter the function results by attribute name. Filter Operator—Choose from a number of operators. Is equal to—The attribute is equal to the value. Does not equal—The attribute is not equal to the value. Is greater than—The attribute is greater than the value. Is greater than or equal to—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. Is less than—The attribute is less than the value. Is less than or equal to—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. Includes the values (bitwise AND equals the value)—bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). Does not include the values (bitwise AND does not equal the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). Includes any (bitwise AND equals True)—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). Does not include any (bitwise AND equals False)—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). Filter Type—Choose from a number of filter types. Specific value—Filter by a specific value. Network attribute—Filter by a network attribute. Filter Value—Provide a specific value for the input filter attribute. Summary Attribute—The field in the Circuit table that will persist the function result. Depending on the specified function and network attribute type, only the applicable type of user-added attributes will be valid for this parameter. If a field to store the summary result does not exist in the Circuit table, the Add Field tool can be used to create one. A field can only support the result of one summary; each summary requires its own field in the Circuit table. See the Usages section for a matrix of valid field types for the summary attribute field based on the specified function. Learn more about bitwise operators"}, {"parameter": "condition_barriers", "label_zh": "條件障礙 （可選）", "label_en": "Condition Barriers (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "The traversability barrier condition that will be set on features based on a comparison to a network attribute or a check for a category string. A condition barrier uses a network attribute, an operator and a type, and an attribute value. For example, stop a trace when a feature has the Device Status attribute equal to the specific value of Open. When a feature meets this condition, the trace stops. If you're using more than one attribute, you can use the Combine Using column to define an And or an Or condition. Value table columns: Name—Filter by any network attribute or network category defined in the system. Operator—Choose from a number of operators. Is equal to—The attribute is equal to the value. Does not equal—The attribute is not equal to the value. Is greater than—The attribute is greater than the value. Is greater than or equal to—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. Is less than—The attribute is less than the value. Is less than or equal to—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. Includes the values (bitwise AND equals the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). Does not include the values (bitwise AND does not equal the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). Includes any (bitwise AND equals True)—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). Does not include any (bitwise AND equals False)—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). Type—Choose a specific value or network attribute from the name value. Specific value—Filter by a specific value or network category. Network attribute—Filter by a network attribute. Value—Provide a specific value for the input attribute type that would cause termination based on the operator value. Combine Using—Set this value if you have multiple attributes to add. You can combine them using an And or an Or condition. And—Combine the condition barriers. Or—Filter by a network attribute."}, {"parameter": "function_barriers", "label_zh": "函數障礙 （可選）", "label_en": "Function Barriers (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "Sets a traversability barrier on features based on a function. Function barriers can be used to do such things as restrict how far the trace travels from the starting point, or set a maximum value to stop a trace. For example, the length of each line traveled is added to the total distance traveled so far. When the total length traveled reaches the value specified, the trace stops. Value table columns: Function—Choose from a number of calculation functions. Minimum—The minimum of the input values. Maximum—The maximum of the input values. Average—The average of the input values. Count—The number of features. Add—The sum of the input values Subtract—The difference between the input values. Attribute—Filter by any network attribute defined in the system. Operator—Choose from a number of operators. Is equal to—The attribute is equal to the value. Does not equal—The attribute is not equal to the value. Is greater than—The attribute is greater than the value. Is greater than or equal to—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. Is less than—The attribute is less than the value. Is less than or equal to—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. Includes the values (bitwise AND equals the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). Does not include the values (bitwise AND does not equal the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). Includes any (bitwise AND equals True)—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). Does not include any (bitwise AND equals False)—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). Value—Provide a specific value of the input attribute type that, if discovered, will cause the termination. Use Local Values—Calculate values in each direction as opposed to an overall global value. For example, use for a function barrier that is calculating the sum of a shape length in which the trace terminates if the value is greater than or equal to 4. In the global case, after you have traversed two edges with a value of 2, you have already reached a shape length sum of 4, so the trace stops. If local values are used, the local values along each path change, and the trace continues."}, {"parameter": "traversability_scope", "label_zh": "應用可遍歷性至 （可選）", "label_en": "Apply Traversability To (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the type of traversability that will be applied. Traversability scope determines whether traversability is applied to junctions, edges, or both. For example, if a condition barrier is defined to stop the trace if Device Status is equal to Open and traversability scope is set to edges only, the trace will not stop even if it encounters an open device, because Device Status is only applicable to junctions. In other words, this parameter indicates to the trace whether to ignore junctions, edges, or both. Both junctions and edges—Traversability will be applied to both junctions and edges. This is the default. Junctions only—Traversability will be applied to junctions only. Edges only—Traversability will be applied to edges only."}, {"parameter": "propagators", "label_zh": "傳播程序 （可選）", "label_en": "Propagators (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "Specifies the network attributes to propagate as well as how that propagation will occur during a trace. Learn more about wavelength propagation Learn more about bitwise operators Value table columns: Attribute—Filter using any network attribute defined in the system. Substitution Attribute—Substitution is not supported with telecom domain networks. Function—Wavelength propagation supports use of the function PROPAGATED_BITWISE_AND. Operator—Choose from the operators INCLUDES_ANY and DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_ANY for wavelength propagation. Value—Provide a specific value for the input attribute type that would cause termination based on the operator value."}, {"parameter": "user_attributes", "label_zh": "用戶屬性 （可選）", "label_en": "User Attributes (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "User-defined attribute fields in the circuit management tables that will mark a circuit as dirty when modified. Value table columns: Circuit Table—The circuit management table containing the user-defined attribute. Attribute Name—The name of a user-defined attribute which will mark the circuit as dirty when modified."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.un.SetCircuitProperties(in_utility_network, domain_network, {share_circuit_locations}, {import_circuits_as_clean}, {max_hops}, {include_containers}, {include_content}, {include_structures}, {include_barriers}, {validate_locatability}, {summaries}, {condition_barriers}, {function_barriers}, {traversability_scope}, {propagators}, {user_attributes})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_utility_network、domain_network、share_circuit_locations、import_circuits_as_clean、max_hops、include_containers、include_content、include_structures、include_barriers、validate_locatability、summaries、condition_barriers、function_barriers、traversability_scope、propagators、user_attributes。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 090184：由于以下原因，所選輪廓線圖徵無效：<value> · 描述

**id**　15c8db17039c935b86-1

**english**　090184: The selected footprint feature is invalid for the following reason: <value>

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 90101-90200

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/090001-100000/tool-errors-and-warnings-90176-90200-090184.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/090001-100000/tool-errors-and-warnings-90176-90200-090184.html

**text**　因為輪廓線圖徵支持 Z 值、與輸入資料集的座標系不匹配或者只包含帶有曲線段的無效圖徵，所以輪廓線圖徵無效。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/090001-100000/tool-errors-and-warnings-90176-90200-090184.html#description

## 090184：由于以下原因，所選輪廓線圖徵無效：<value> · 解決方案

**id**　15c8db17039c935b86-2

**english**　090184: The selected footprint feature is invalid for the following reason: <value>

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 90101-90200

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/090001-100000/tool-errors-and-warnings-90176-90200-090184.html#solution
